Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. You may only edit: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`, `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   No approximations are allowed for these opcodes.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel. CPU detection is already done\n   once at start-up; never detect features per block or per sample.\n6. Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t    float operator()(std::size_t n) const {\n   102\t        if (g_seed) return noise[n];\n   103\t        constexpr std::size_t total = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE;\n   104\t        constexpr std::size_t quarter = total / 4;\n   105\t        std::size_t seg = n / quarter, k = n % quarter;\n   106\t        float t = static_cast<float>(k) / static_cast<float>(quarter);\n   107\t        switch (seg) {\n   108\t            case 0: return gain * noise[n];\n   109\t            case 1: {\n   110\t                // 20 Hz \u2192 20 kHz exponential sweep, phase-integrated\n   111\t                double f0 = 20.0, f1 = 20000.0, T = double(quarter) / double(SR);\n   112\t                double tt = double(k) / double(SR);\n   113\t                double kk = std::log(f1 / f0) / T;\n   114\t                double ph = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * f0 * (std::exp(kk * tt) - 1.0) / kk;\n   115\t                return gain * static_cast<float>(std::sin(ph));\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            case 2: return (k % 997 == 0) ? gain : 0.0f;\n   118\t            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n   119\t        }\n   120\t    }\n   121\t};\n   122\t\n   123\tStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n   124\t    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n   125\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n   126\t        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n   127\t        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n   128\t        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n   129\t    }\n   130\t    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n   131\t}\n   132\tStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n   133\t\n   134\tstruct Case {\n   135\t    const char* name;\n   136\t    Opcode op;\n   137\t    std::vector<Stim> inputs;      // wired to inputs[0..]; remaining slots NONE\n   138\t    std::uint16_t first_unused = 0; // inputs >= this index are NONE\n   139\t};\n   140\t\n   141\tstd::vector<Case> make_cases() {\n   142\t    AudioStim audio(1.0f), hot(3.0f);\n   143\t    constexpr std::size_t P = 48000; The event-transforms PRD requires closure handles stored in `StateInitData` so its `EVENT_MAP` opcode can dispatch the closure per event. To satisfy that, this PRD relaxes the restriction *only* for: (1) BlockRefs consumed directly by an `EVENT_MAP` / `EVENT_FILTER` / `FOREACH_EVENT` opcode at the call site they were declared in, (2) the BlockRef is stored as a `BlockId + capture-vector` reference in `StateInitData`, not as a heap-allocated value. The \"no escape across hot-swap boundaries\" and \"no return from a fn\" restrictions stand; only the \"no storage in StateInitData\" restriction lifts. Concretely: `event_map(events, (e) -> {...})` is supported; `fn make_xform() = (e) -> {...}` returning a closure is not.\ndocs/prd-stereo-support.md:493: `my_fx = fn(sig) -> sig |> filter_lp(@, 500, 0.7) |> delay(@, 0.2, 0.5)` \u2014 the intended semantics is that `my_fx(mono_in)` produces `Mono` and `my_fx(stereo_in)` produces `Stereo`, with channel-type polymorphism inherited from the body's auto-lift behaviour. The exact inference rule (e.g. parametric channel-type variables, monomorphisation-per-callsite, or something simpler) is an **open question (OQ5)** to resolve jointly with [`prd-advanced-functions`](prd-advanced-functions.md). Bit identity is preserved:\\n// add/sub/mul are fully associative for finite operands, and AVX2/SSE perform\\n// NaN/zero/denormal arithmetic with identical semantics to the scalar x87/SSE\\n// fallback.\\ntemplate <typename Op>\\n[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void map3(const float* a, const float* b, float* out, Op op) noexcept {\\n    static_assert(BLOCK_SIZE == 128 && BLOCK_SIZE % 32 == 0, \\\"avx2 map3 kernel expects 128 samples\\\");\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(a, 32);\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(b, 32);\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(out, 32);\\n\\n    // `out` may alias one input by (BLOCK_SIZE - i) samples. Advance each\\n    // vector by that displacement so every 8-element copy either overlaps\\n    // itself by a whole vector (safe: out[j]=av[j] op bv[j]) or uses only\\n    // already-written lanes (safe: overlapping tail lanes have their final\\n    // value). Loading at the same relative displacement is therefore always\\n    // source-correct.\\n    std::size_t o = 0;\\n    std::size_t ao = 0;\\n    std::size_t bo = 0;\\n    if (out == a + (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) ao = 8;\\n    if (out == b + (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) bo = 8;\\n    if (out == a - (BLOCK_SIZE - 8) || out == a + 2 * (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) ao = 120;\\n    if (out == b - (BLOCK_SIZE - 8) || out == b + 2 * (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) bo = 120;\\n\\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8) {\\n        const std::size_t k = (i + o) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        const std::size_t ka = (i + ao) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        const std::size_t kb = (i + bo) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + k, op(_mm256_load_ps(a + ka), _mm256_load_ps(b + kb)));\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nstruct Add { [[gnu::always_inline]] vf operator()(vf x, vf y) const noexcept { return _mm256_add_ps(x, y); } };\\nstruct Sub { [[gnu::always_inline]] vf operator()(vf x, vf y) const noexcept { return _mm256_sub_ps(x, y); } };\\nstruct Mul { [[gnu::always_inline]] vf operator()(vf x, vf y) const noexcept { return _mm256_mul_ps(x, y); } };\\n\\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void add_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\\n    map3(a, b, out, Add{});\\n}\\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void sub_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\\n    map3(a, b, out, Sub{});\\n}\\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\\n    map3(a, b, out, Mul{});\\n}\\n}  // namespace avx2\\n\\ninline void add_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept { avx2::add_avx2(a, b, out); }\\ninline void sub_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept { avx2::sub_avx2(a, b, out); }\\ninline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept { avx2::mul_avx2(a, b, out); }\\n#endif\\n}  // namespace kernels\"}"

Bit identity is preserved:\\n// add/sub/mul are fully associative for finite operands, and AVX2/SSE perform\\n// NaN/zero/denormal arithmetic with identical semantics to the scalar x87/SSE\\n// fallback.\\ntemplate <typename Op>\\n[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void map3(const float* a, const float* b, float* out, Op op) noexcept {\\n    static_assert(BLOCK_SIZE == 128 && BLOCK_SIZE % 32 == 0, \\\"avx2 map3 kernel expects 128 samples\\\");\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(a, 32);\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(b, 32);\\n    CEDAR_SIMD_ASSERT_ALIGNED(out, 32);\\n\\n    // `out` may alias one input by (BLOCK_SIZE - i) samples. Advance each\\n    // vector by that displacement so every 8-element copy either overlaps\\n    // itself by a whole vector (safe: out[j]=av[j] op bv[j]) or uses only\\n    // already-written lanes (safe: overlapping tail lanes have their final\\n    // value). Loading at the same relative displacement is therefore always\\n    // source-correct.\\n    std::size_t o = 0;\\n    std::size_t ao = 0;\\n    std::size_t bo = 0;\\n    if (out == a + (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) ao = 8;\\n    if (out == b + (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) bo = 8;\\n    if (out == a - (BLOCK_SIZE - 8) || out == a + 2 * (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) ao = 120;\\n    if (out == b - (BLOCK_SIZE - 8) || out == b + 2 * (BLOCK_SIZE - 8)) bo = 120;\\n\\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8) {\\n        const std::size_t k = (i + o) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        const std::size_t ka = (i + ao) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        const std::size_t kb = (i + bo) & (BLOCK_SIZE - 1);\\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + k, op(_mm256_load_ps(a + ka), _mm256_load_ps(b + kb)));\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nstruct Add { CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 [[gnu::always_inline]] vf operator()(vf x, vf y) const noexcept { return _mm256_add_ps(x, y);
